ABOUTME: Card #1459 process record: every process that counts, one per line, with host, pid, creation time and the UTC time it was recorded.
Names masked under the owner's ruling (1459#7090274068). The original is kept off-repo; its sha256 is withheld for privacy (1459#7104456293); generated before masking.
Generated by source/gen-record.py from transcript.txt beside this file. Each line starts with the step whose transcript entry printed it.
WSL entries: boot= is the VM's boot id and starttime= is field 22 of /proc/<pid>/stat (clock ticks since that boot); together they are the
creation time as the kernel reports it, and created= is the UTC time derived from them (supervisor, writer and member lines omit created=).
Windows entries: created= is Win32_Process.CreationDate in UTC. recorded= is when the entry was written. Member lines were written into the
run's members.txt by the process that started the member, as it started it. A member's boot id is that of the supervisor execution that
started it: the latest supervisor record of the same run name before the member, so rl-n2 and rs-n2 keep rlb's and rsb's boot apart from
rlc's and rsc's (round 1 of this record wrongly gave all sixteen of rlb's and rsb's member lines the later boot; step c3 checks the fix).
VOID (note written 2026-11-22 by step s2 into the WSL record file, and kept here): the eight step=s1 lines labelled "end:" for pids 649, 677,
678, 686, 693, 701, 702 and 706 are [name withheld]'s SSH login as [name withheld] and [name withheld]'s user manager, not the spike's. s1's scan recorded every new process
in user.slice; the scan was narrowed before s2. They are kept, not deleted, and are no part of the spike's record.
The REC lines are the record. The UNRECORDED lines at the end are not: they are the processes the spike started that count under the card's
"Every pid, recorded at its start" but were not recorded at their start. That was required and the spike failed to do it; the gap cannot be
recovered. Each line gives the pid and creation time where a listing printed them, and why it was not recorded.
